+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
+               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");
+
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
+    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
+    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);
+
+    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
+    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
+    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
+                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
+    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
+        groupSize = 128;
+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }
+    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)} * 8));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {
+        if (rows == 0) return;
+        switch (groupSize) {
+        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
+            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
+                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
+        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
+            case 128:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };
+
+    if (iterations != 0) {
+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {

+    }

+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
+// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
+template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
+__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
+    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
+    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
+    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
+                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
+    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
+    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
+// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
+template <unsigned BlockSize>
+__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
+    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) : bytes_(count * sizeof(T)) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), bytes_), "cudaMalloc");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* data() const { return data_; }
+    void upload(const T* source, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(data_, source, bytes_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+                      "copy input to GPU");
+        }
+    }
+    void download(T* destination, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(destination, data_, bytes_, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "copy output from GPU");
+        }
+    }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t bytes_;
+};
+
+struct CudaStream {
+    cudaStream_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&handle, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                            "create CUDA stream"); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaEvent {
+    cudaEvent_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaEvent() { checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&handle), "create CUDA event"); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaGraph {
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CudaGraph() = default;
+    ~CudaGraph() {
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
+    }
+    CudaGraph(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+    CudaGraph& operator=(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+};
+
+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
+               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");
+
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
+    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
+    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);
+
+    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
+    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
+    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
+                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
+    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
+        groupSize = 128;
+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }
+    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)} * 8));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {
+        if (rows == 0) return;
+        switch (groupSize) {
+        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
+            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
+                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
+        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
+            case 128:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };
+
+    if (iterations != 0) {
+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
+            remaining -= batch;
+        }
+    }
+    if (rows != 0) {
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}
+
 // ****************************************************************************
 // Function: verifyResults
 //

The first GPU runs pass validation. For 100 iterations of the 4096×4096 case, the measured SpMV time dropped from 248 ms on the CPU to about 3.45 ms on the GPU. I’ll now check the remaining kernel paths and use CUDA’s memory and synchronization checks to catch errors validation might miss.
exec
/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import math
import re
import subprocess

cpu = '/tmp/spmv_cuda_reference'
gpu = '/tmp/spmv_cuda_build/spmv'
cases = [
    (0, 10, 1, 1), (1, 1, 1, 1), (1, 2, 3, 1), (7, 100, 3, 1),
    (7, 1, 9, 1), (31, 16, 2, 1), (33, 12, 10, 1), (127, 20, 129, 1),
    (129, 10, 130, 1), (257, 5, 128, 1), (513, 4, 9, 1), (1024, 2, 129, 1),
    (2049, 1, 3, 1), (4096, 10, 100, 1), (8192, 100, 129, 1),
    (257, 1, 3, 0), (33, 2, 3, -3), (33, 2, 0, 1),
]
keys = re.compile(r'"'^(Sum|Min|Max|Sample'"\\[\\d+\\]): (.+)"'$'"', re.M)
max_error = 0.0
for n, s, iterations, maximum in cases:
    args = ['-n', str(n), '-s', str(s), '-i', str(iterations), '-m', str(maximum), '-r']
    reference = subprocess.run([cpu, *args], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout
    result = subprocess.run([gpu, *args, *([] if iterations == 0 else ['-v'])], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout
    expected = {key: float(value) for key, value in keys.findall(reference)}
    actual = {key: float(value) for key, value in keys.findall(result)}
    assert expected.keys() == actual.keys(), (n, s, expected.keys(), actual.keys())
    for key, value in expected.items():
        assert math.isclose(value, actual[key], rel_tol=5e-13, abs_tol=1e-12), (n, s, key, value, actual[key])
        if value:
            max_error = max(max_error, abs(actual[key] - value) / abs(value))
    assert 'Elements: ' + str(n) in result
    assert iterations == 0 or 'Validation: PASSED' in result
    print(f'PASS n={n}, sparsity={s}, iterations={iterations}, max={maximum}', flush=True)
print(f'All {len(cases)} CPU/GPU comparisons passed; maximum relative difference {max_error:.3e}.')

+// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
+// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
+template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
+__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
+    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
+    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
+    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
+                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
+    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
+    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
+// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
+template <unsigned BlockSize>
+__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
+    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) : bytes_(count * sizeof(T)) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), bytes_), "cudaMalloc");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* data() const { return data_; }
+    void upload(const T* source, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(data_, source, bytes_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+                      "copy input to GPU");
+        }
+    }
+    void download(T* destination, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(destination, data_, bytes_, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "copy output from GPU");
+        }
+    }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t bytes_;
+};
+
+struct CudaStream {
+    cudaStream_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&handle, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                            "create CUDA stream"); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaEvent {
+    cudaEvent_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaEvent() { checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&handle), "create CUDA event"); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaGraph {
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CudaGraph() = default;
+    ~CudaGraph() {
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
+    }
+    CudaGraph(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+    CudaGraph& operator=(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+};
+
+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
+               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");
+
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
+    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
+    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);
+
+    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
+    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
+    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
+                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
+    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
+        groupSize = 128;
+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }
+    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)} * 8));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {
+        if (rows == 0) return;
+        switch (groupSize) {
+        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
+            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
+                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
+        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
+            case 128:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };
+
+    if (iterations != 0) {
+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
+            remaining -= batch;
+        }
+    }
+    if (rows != 0) {
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
+// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
+template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
+__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
+    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
+    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
+    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
+                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
+    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
+    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
+// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
+template <unsigned BlockSize>
+__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
+    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) : bytes_(count * sizeof(T)) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), bytes_), "cudaMalloc");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* data() const { return data_; }
+    void upload(const T* source, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(data_, source, bytes_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+                      "copy input to GPU");
+        }
+    }
+    void download(T* destination, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(destination, data_, bytes_, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "copy output from GPU");
+        }
+    }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t bytes_;
+};
+
+struct CudaStream {
+    cudaStream_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&handle, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                            "create CUDA stream"); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaEvent {
+    cudaEvent_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaEvent() { checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&handle), "create CUDA event"); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaGraph {
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CudaGraph() = default;
+    ~CudaGraph() {
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
+    }
+    CudaGraph(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+    CudaGraph& operator=(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+};
+
+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
+               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");
+
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
+    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
+    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);
+
+    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
+    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
+    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
+                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
+    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
+        groupSize = 128;
+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }
+    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)} * 8));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {
+        if (rows == 0) return;
+        switch (groupSize) {
+        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
+            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
+                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
+        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
+            case 128:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };
+
+    if (iterations != 0) {
+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
+            remaining -= batch;
+        }
+    }
+    if (rows != 0) {
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
+// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
+template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
+__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
+    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
+    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
+    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
+                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
+    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
+    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
+// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
+template <unsigned BlockSize>
+__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
+    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}

+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,

+    int device = 0;

+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);

+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8

+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }

+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)} * 8));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {

+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };

+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
+            remaining -= batch;
+        }
+    }
+    if (rows != 0) {
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
+// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
+template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
+__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
+    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
+    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
+    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
+                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
+    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
+    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
+// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
+template <unsigned BlockSize>
+__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
+    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) : bytes_(count * sizeof(T)) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), bytes_), "cudaMalloc");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* data() const { return data_; }
+    void upload(const T* source, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(data_, source, bytes_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+                      "copy input to GPU");
+        }
+    }
+    void download(T* destination, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(destination, data_, bytes_, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "copy output from GPU");
+        }
+    }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t bytes_;
+};
+
+struct CudaStream {
+    cudaStream_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&handle, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                            "create CUDA stream"); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaEvent {
+    cudaEvent_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaEvent() { checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&handle), "create CUDA event"); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaGraph {
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CudaGraph() = default;
+    ~CudaGraph() {
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
+    }
+    CudaGraph(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+    CudaGraph& operator=(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+};
+
+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
+               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");
+
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
+    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
+    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);
+
+    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
+    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
+    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
+                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
+    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
+        groupSize = 128;
+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }
+    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)} * 8));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {
+        if (rows == 0) return;
+        switch (groupSize) {
+        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
+            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
+                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
+        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
+            case 128:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };
+
+    if (iterations != 0) {
+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
+            remaining -= batch;
+        }
+    }
+    if (rows != 0) {
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}
+

//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;
//                  last element is the index one past the last element
//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication
//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix
//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation
//
// ****************************************************************************
void spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,
             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {
        double t = 0.0;
        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {
            const auto col = cols[j];
            t += val[j] * vec[col];
        }
        out[i] = t;
    }
}

// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
        double sum = 0.0;
        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
        }
        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
        }
        if (lane == 0) {
            out[row] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
template <unsigned BlockSize>
__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
        double sum = 0.0;
        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
        }
        #pragma unroll
        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
        }
        if (lane == 0) {
            warpSums[warp] = sum;
        }
        __syncthreads();
        if (warp == 0) {
            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
            #pragma unroll
            for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
            }
            if (lane == 0) {
                out[row] = sum;
            }
        }
        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
        __syncthreads();
    }
}

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

template <typename T>
class DeviceBuffer {
public:
    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) : bytes_(count * sizeof(T)) {
        if (bytes_ != 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), bytes_), "cudaMalloc");
        }
    }
    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
    T* data() const { return data_; }
    void upload(const T* source, cudaStream_t stream) {
        if (bytes_ != 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(data_, source, bytes_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
                      "copy input to GPU");
        }
    }
    void download(T* destination, cudaStream_t stream) {
        if (bytes_ != 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(destination, data_, bytes_, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
                      "copy output from GPU");
        }
    }
private:
    T* data_ = nullptr;
    size_t bytes_;
};

                            "create CUDA stream"); }

        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);

    CudaGraph& operator=(const CudaGraph&) = delete;

double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
    int device = 0;
    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");

    CudaStream stream;
    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);

    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
        groupSize = 128;
    }
    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
        groupSize = 256;
    }
    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)} * 8));

    const auto launch = [&]() {
        if (rows == 0) return;
        switch (groupSize) {
        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
            case 128:
                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
                break;
            case 256:
                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
                break;
        }
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
    };

    if (iterations != 0) {
        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
    } else if (rows != 0) {
        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
    }
    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");

    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
    CudaGraph graph;
    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
    }

    CudaEvent start, stop;
    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
    index_t remaining = iterations;
    if (graph.executable) {
        while (remaining >= batch) {
            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
            remaining -= batch;
        }
    }
    if (rows != 0) {
        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
    }
    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
    return milliseconds;
}

// ****************************************************************************
// Function: verifyResults
//
// Purpose:
//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution
//
// Arguments:
//   reference: array holding the reference result vector
//   result: array holding the result vector to verify
//   size: number of elements per vector
//
// ****************************************************************************
bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {
    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
        const double ref = reference[i];
        const double res = result[i];
        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
            // For very small values, check absolute error
            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n", i, ref, res);
                return false;
            }
        } else {
            // Check relative error
            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
                printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, ref, res, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    return true;
}

+// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
+// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
+template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
+__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
+    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
+    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
+    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
+                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
+    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
+    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
+// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
+template <unsigned BlockSize>
+__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
+    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) : bytes_(count * sizeof(T)) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), bytes_), "cudaMalloc");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* data() const { return data_; }
+    void upload(const T* source, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(data_, source, bytes_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+                      "copy input to GPU");
+        }
+    }
+    void download(T* destination, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(destination, data_, bytes_, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "copy output from GPU");
+        }
+    }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t bytes_;
+};
+
+struct CudaStream {
+    cudaStream_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&handle, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                            "create CUDA stream"); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaEvent {
+    cudaEvent_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaEvent() { checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&handle), "create CUDA event"); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaGraph {
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CudaGraph() = default;
+    ~CudaGraph() {
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
+    }
+    CudaGraph(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+    CudaGraph& operator=(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+};
+
+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
+               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");
+
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
+    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
+    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);
+
+    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
+    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
+    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
+                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
+    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
+        groupSize = 128;
+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }
+    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)} * 8));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {
+        if (rows == 0) return;
+        switch (groupSize) {
+        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
+            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
+                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
+        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
+            case 128:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };
+
+    if (iterations != 0) {
+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
+            remaining -= batch;
+        }
+    }
+    if (rows != 0) {
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}
