int main() {
    const std::pair<index_t, index_t> cases[] = {
        {67, 1}, {71, 2}, {73, 4}, {79, 8}, {83, 16}, {89, 32},
        {1003, 300}, {2053, 2300}, {4099, 2300}, {200003, 1}
    };
    for (const auto [rows, length] : cases) {
        std::vector<double> val, vec(rows), reference(rows), output(rows);
        std::vector<index_t> cols, delimiters(size_t{rows} + 1);
        for (index_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
            vec[row] = (static_cast<int>(row % 33) - 16) * 0.0625;
            delimiters[row] = static_cast<index_t>(val.size());
            const index_t count = row % 17 == 0 ? 0 : length;
            for (index_t j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
                cols.push_back(static_cast<index_t>((uint64_t{row} * 7 + j * 17) % rows));
                val.push_back((static_cast<int>((j + row) % 65) - 32) * 0.03125);
            }
        }
        delimiters[rows] = static_cast<index_t>(val.size());
        spmvCpu(val.data(), cols.data(), delimiters.data(), vec.data(), rows, reference.data());
        for (index_t iterations : {1u, 129u}) {
            spmvGpu(val, cols, delimiters, vec, iterations, output);
            for (index_t row = 0; row < rows; ++row) {
                if (reference[row] "'!= output[row]) {
                    fprintf(stderr, "Mismatch: rows=%u length=%u iterations=%u row=%u expected=%.17g actual=%.17g'"\\n\",
                            rows, length, iterations, row, reference[row], output[row]);
                    return 1;
                }
            }
        }
        printf(\"All rows match exactly: rows=%u, nominal row length=%u, empty and signed rows, iterations=1/129\\n\", rows, length);
    }
    return 0;
}

+// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
+// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
+template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
+__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
+    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
+    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
+    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
+                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
+    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
+    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
+// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
+template <unsigned BlockSize>
+__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
+    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned offset = BlockSize / 64; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
+        if (row + gridDim.x < dim) {
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) : bytes_(count * sizeof(T)) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), bytes_), "cudaMalloc");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* data() const { return data_; }
+    void upload(const T* source, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(data_, source, bytes_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+                      "copy input to GPU");
+        }
+    }
+    void download(T* destination, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(destination, data_, bytes_, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "copy output from GPU");
+        }
+    }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t bytes_;
+};
+
+struct CudaStream {
+    cudaStream_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&handle, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                            "create CUDA stream"); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaEvent {
+    cudaEvent_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaEvent() { checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&handle), "create CUDA event"); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaGraph {
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CudaGraph() = default;
+    ~CudaGraph() {
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
+    }
+    CudaGraph(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+    CudaGraph& operator=(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+};
+
+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
+               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");
+
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
+    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
+    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);
+
+    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
+    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
+    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
+                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
+    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
+        groupSize = 128;
+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }
+    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)}
+                    * (groupSize > 32 ? 16 : 8)));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {
+        if (rows == 0) return;
+        switch (groupSize) {
+        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
+            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
+                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
+        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
+            case 128:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };
+
+    if (iterations != 0) {
+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
+            remaining -= batch;
+        }
+    }
+    if (rows != 0) {
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
+// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
+template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
+__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
+    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
+    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
+    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
+                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
+    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
+    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
+// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
+template <unsigned BlockSize>
+__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
+    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned offset = BlockSize / 64; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
+        if (row + gridDim.x < dim) {
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) : bytes_(count * sizeof(T)) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), bytes_), "cudaMalloc");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* data() const { return data_; }
+    void upload(const T* source, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(data_, source, bytes_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+                      "copy input to GPU");
+        }
+    }
+    void download(T* destination, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(destination, data_, bytes_, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "copy output from GPU");
+        }
+    }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t bytes_;
+};
+
+struct CudaStream {
+    cudaStream_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&handle, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                            "create CUDA stream"); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaEvent {
+    cudaEvent_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaEvent() { checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&handle), "create CUDA event"); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaGraph {
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CudaGraph() = default;
+    ~CudaGraph() {
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
+    }
+    CudaGraph(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+    CudaGraph& operator=(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+};
+
+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
+               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");
+
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
+    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
+    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);
+
+    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
+    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
+    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
+                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
+    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
+        groupSize = 128;
+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }
+    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)}
+                    * (groupSize > 32 ? 16 : 8)));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {
+        if (rows == 0) return;
+        switch (groupSize) {
+        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
+            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
+                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
+        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
+            case 128:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };
+
+    if (iterations != 0) {
+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
+            remaining -= batch;
+        }
+    }
+    if (rows != 0) {
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
+// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
+template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
+__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
+    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
+    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
+    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
+                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
+    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
+    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
+// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
+template <unsigned BlockSize>
+__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
+    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned offset = BlockSize / 64; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
+        if (row + gridDim.x < dim) {
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+

+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* data() const { return data_; }
+    void upload(const T* source, cudaStream_t stream) {

+                      "copy input to GPU");
+        }

+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(destination, data_, bytes_, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "copy output from GPU");
+        }
+    }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t bytes_;
+};

+struct CudaGraph {

+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
+               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");
+
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
+    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
+    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);
+
+    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
+    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
+    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
+                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
+    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
+        groupSize = 128;
+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }
+    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)}
+                    * (groupSize > 32 ? 16 : 8)));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {
+        if (rows == 0) return;
+        switch (groupSize) {
+        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
+            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
+                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
+        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
+            case 128:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };
+
+    if (iterations != 0) {
+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
+            remaining -= batch;
+        }
+    }
+    if (rows != 0) {
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
+// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
+template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
+__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
+    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
+    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
+    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
+                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
+    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
+    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
+// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
+template <unsigned BlockSize>
+__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
+    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned offset = BlockSize / 64; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
+        if (row + gridDim.x < dim) {
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) : bytes_(count * sizeof(T)) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), bytes_), "cudaMalloc");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* data() const { return data_; }
+    void upload(const T* source, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(data_, source, bytes_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+                      "copy input to GPU");
+        }
+    }
+    void download(T* destination, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(destination, data_, bytes_, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "copy output from GPU");
+        }
+    }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t bytes_;
+};
+
+struct CudaStream {
+    cudaStream_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&handle, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                            "create CUDA stream"); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaEvent {
+    cudaEvent_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaEvent() { checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&handle), "create CUDA event"); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaGraph {
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CudaGraph() = default;
+    ~CudaGraph() {
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
+    }
+    CudaGraph(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+    CudaGraph& operator=(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+};
+
+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
+               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");
+
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
+    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
+    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);
+
+    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
+    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
+    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
+                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
+    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
+        groupSize = 128;
+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }
+    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)}
+                    * (groupSize > 32 ? 16 : 8)));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {
+        if (rows == 0) return;
+        switch (groupSize) {
+        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
+            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
+                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
+        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
+            case 128:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };
+
+    if (iterations != 0) {
+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
+            remaining -= batch;
+        }
+    }
+    if (rows != 0) {
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}

+// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
+// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
+template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
+__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
+    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
+    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
+    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
+                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
+    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
+    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
+// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
+template <unsigned BlockSize>
+__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
+    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned offset = BlockSize / 64; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
+        if (row + gridDim.x < dim) {
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) : bytes_(count * sizeof(T)) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), bytes_), "cudaMalloc");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* data() const { return data_; }
+    void upload(const T* source, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(data_, source, bytes_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+                      "copy input to GPU");
+        }
+    }
+    void download(T* destination, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(destination, data_, bytes_, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "copy output from GPU");
+        }
+    }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t bytes_;
+};
+
+struct CudaStream {
+    cudaStream_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&handle, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                            "create CUDA stream"); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaEvent {
+    cudaEvent_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaEvent() { checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&handle), "create CUDA event"); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaGraph {
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CudaGraph() = default;
+    ~CudaGraph() {
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
+    }
+    CudaGraph(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+    CudaGraph& operator=(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+};
+
+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
+               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");
+
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
+    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
+    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);
+
+    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
+    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
+    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
+                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
+    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
+        groupSize = 128;
+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }
+    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)}
+                    * (groupSize > 32 ? 16 : 8)));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {
+        if (rows == 0) return;
+        switch (groupSize) {
+        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
+            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
+                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
+        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
+            case 128:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };
+
+    if (iterations != 0) {
+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
+            remaining -= batch;
+        }
+    }
+    if (rows != 0) {
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}

Implemented unconditional CUDA SpMV with adaptive double-precision kernels, GPU-resident buffers, and CUDA graphs. Updated CMake; the executable remains `spmv`. Only existing source files were changed.

CPU comparisons and CUDA memory, race, and synchronization checks passed.

On the RTX 3090, the 4096×4096 case completed 100 iterations in **3.46 ms versus 248 ms**—about **72× faster computation**.

+// Each subgroup owns a row. Adjacent lanes read adjacent CSR entries, and
+// shuffles reduce the double-precision partial sums without atomics.
+template <unsigned ThreadsPerRow>
+__global__ void spmvRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                               const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                               const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                               const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & (ThreadsPerRow - 1);
+    constexpr unsigned subgroupMask = 0xffffffffu >> (32 - ThreadsPerRow);
+    const unsigned mask = subgroupMask << ((threadIdx.x & 31) & ~(ThreadsPerRow - 1));
+    const size_t firstRow = size_t{blockIdx.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow)
+                          + threadIdx.x / ThreadsPerRow;
+    const size_t rowStride = size_t{gridDim.x} * (blockDim.x / ThreadsPerRow);
+    for (size_t row = firstRow; row < dim; row += rowStride) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + lane; j < end; j += ThreadsPerRow) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = ThreadsPerRow / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(mask, sum, offset, ThreadsPerRow);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            out[row] = sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Long rows use an entire block to expose enough parallel work even when the
+// matrix has relatively few rows. The last reduction uses just one warp.
+template <unsigned BlockSize>
+__global__ void spmvLongRowsKernel(const double* __restrict__ val,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+                                   const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+                                   const double* __restrict__ vec,
+                                   const index_t dim, double* __restrict__ out) {
+    __shared__ double warpSums[BlockSize / 32];
+    const unsigned lane = threadIdx.x & 31;
+    const unsigned warp = threadIdx.x / 32;
+    for (size_t row = blockIdx.x; row < dim; row += gridDim.x) {
+        const index_t begin = rowDelimiters[row];
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        for (size_t j = size_t{begin} + threadIdx.x; j < end; j += BlockSize) {
+            sum += val[j] * vec[cols[j]];
+        }
+        #pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned offset = 16; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+            sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+        }
+        if (lane == 0) {
+            warpSums[warp] = sum;
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (warp == 0) {
+            sum = lane < BlockSize / 32 ? warpSums[lane] : 0.0;
+            #pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned offset = BlockSize / 64; offset > 0; offset /= 2) {
+                sum += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, sum, offset);
+            }
+            if (lane == 0) {
+                out[row] = sum;
+            }
+        }
+        // Protect shared partial sums before this block advances to another row.
+        if (row + gridDim.x < dim) {
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+class DeviceBuffer {
+public:
+    explicit DeviceBuffer(size_t count) : bytes_(count * sizeof(T)) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&data_), bytes_), "cudaMalloc");
+        }
+    }
+    ~DeviceBuffer() { if (data_) cudaFree(data_); }
+    DeviceBuffer(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    DeviceBuffer& operator=(const DeviceBuffer&) = delete;
+    T* data() const { return data_; }
+    void upload(const T* source, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(data_, source, bytes_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream),
+                      "copy input to GPU");
+        }
+    }
+    void download(T* destination, cudaStream_t stream) {
+        if (bytes_ != 0) {
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(destination, data_, bytes_, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream),
+                      "copy output from GPU");
+        }
+    }
+private:
+    T* data_ = nullptr;
+    size_t bytes_;
+};
+
+struct CudaStream {
+    cudaStream_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&handle, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                            "create CUDA stream"); }
+    ~CudaStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaStream(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+    CudaStream& operator=(const CudaStream&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaEvent {
+    cudaEvent_t handle = nullptr;
+    CudaEvent() { checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&handle), "create CUDA event"); }
+    ~CudaEvent() { cudaEventDestroy(handle); }
+    CudaEvent(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+    CudaEvent& operator=(const CudaEvent&) = delete;
+};
+
+struct CudaGraph {
+    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
+    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
+    CudaGraph() = default;
+    ~CudaGraph() {
+        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
+        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
+    }
+    CudaGraph(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+    CudaGraph& operator=(const CudaGraph&) = delete;
+};
+
+double spmvGpu(const std::vector<double>& val, const std::vector<index_t>& cols,
+               const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters, const std::vector<double>& vec,
+               const index_t iterations, std::vector<double>& out) {
+    int device = 0;
+    cudaDeviceProp properties{};
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "get CUDA device");
+    checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "query CUDA device");
+
+    CudaStream stream;
+    DeviceBuffer<double> d_val(val.size()), d_vec(vec.size()), d_out(out.size());
+    DeviceBuffer<index_t> d_cols(cols.size()), d_rows(rowDelimiters.size());
+    d_val.upload(val.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_cols.upload(cols.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_rows.upload(rowDelimiters.data(), stream.handle);
+    d_vec.upload(vec.data(), stream.handle);
+
+    const index_t rows = static_cast<index_t>(vec.size());
+    const double averageLength = rows != 0 ? static_cast<double>(val.size()) / rows : 0.0;
+    unsigned groupSize = averageLength <= 1 ? 1 : averageLength <= 2 ? 2
+                       : averageLength <= 4 ? 4 : averageLength <= 8 ? 8
+                       : averageLength <= 16 ? 16 : 32;
+    if (averageLength >= 256 && rows <= unsigned(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 16) {
+        groupSize = 128;
+    }
+    if (averageLength >= 2048) {
+        groupSize = 256;
+    }
+    const unsigned blockSize = groupSize > 32 ? groupSize : 256;
+    const size_t neededBlocks = (size_t{rows} + blockSize / groupSize - 1)
+                             / (blockSize / groupSize);
+    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+        neededBlocks, size_t{static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount)}
+                    * (groupSize > 32 ? 16 : 8)));
+
+    const auto launch = [&]() {
+        if (rows == 0) return;
+        switch (groupSize) {
+        #define LAUNCH_ROWS(N) \
+            case N: spmvRowsKernel<N><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream.handle>>>( \
+                d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data()); break
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(1);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(2);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(4);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(8);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(16);
+            LAUNCH_ROWS(32);
+        #undef LAUNCH_ROWS
+            case 128:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<128><<<blocks, 128, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+            case 256:
+                spmvLongRowsKernel<256><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream.handle>>>(
+                    d_val.data(), d_cols.data(), d_rows.data(), d_vec.data(), rows, d_out.data());
+                break;
+        }
+        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "launch SpMV kernel");
+    };
+
+    if (iterations != 0) {
+        launch(); // Warm up before measuring; input transfers are also outside the timer.
+    } else if (rows != 0) {
+        checkCuda(cudaMemsetAsync(d_out.data(), 0, out.size() * sizeof(double), stream.handle),
+                  "initialize output for zero iterations");
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare GPU inputs");
+
+    // Replay a bounded chain of kernels to reduce launch overhead for short
+    // matrices. Each requested iteration still performs a complete SpMV.
+    CudaGraph graph;
+    const index_t batch = std::min<index_t>(iterations, 128);
+    if (rows != 0 && batch > 1) {
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream.handle, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal),
+                  "begin CUDA graph capture");
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < batch; ++i) launch();
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream.handle, &graph.graph), "finish CUDA graph capture");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&graph.executable, graph.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0),
+                  "instantiate CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(graph.executable, stream.handle), "upload CUDA graph");
+        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "prepare CUDA graph");
+    }
+
+    CudaEvent start, stop;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(start.handle, stream.handle), "record start time");
+    index_t remaining = iterations;
+    if (graph.executable) {
+        while (remaining >= batch) {
+            checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(graph.executable, stream.handle), "launch CUDA graph");
+            remaining -= batch;
+        }
+    }
+    if (rows != 0) {
+        for (index_t i = 0; i < remaining; ++i) launch();
+    }
+    checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(stop.handle, stream.handle), "record stop time");
+    checkCuda(cudaEventSynchronize(stop.handle), "wait for SpMV completion");
+    float milliseconds = 0.0f;
+    checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&milliseconds, start.handle, stop.handle), "measure SpMV time");
+    d_out.download(out.data(), stream.handle);
+    checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream.handle), "wait for output transfer");
+    return milliseconds;
+}
+

Implemented unconditional CUDA SpMV with adaptive double-precision kernels, GPU-resident buffers, and CUDA graphs. Updated CMake; the executable remains `spmv`. Only existing source files were changed.

CPU comparisons and CUDA memory, race, and synchronization checks passed.

On the RTX 3090, the 4096×4096 case completed 100 iterations in **3.46 ms versus 248 ms**—about **72× faster computation**.
